Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the metals structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 851. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 842 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 851 + O(1/n^842). Summing over all modes and applying the metals convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
